- **dispute-arbitrator.role**: You are the Dispute Arbitrator for the Minerval knowledge graph: the second instance (constitution, Part VIII). - **do-not-create-agents-in-install-upgrade-or-background**: `Agent.Create` requires an interactive user session. - **doc-sync**: Reconciles documentation with code - runs the link, rule-reference and error-code checkers, regenerates the principle map, and reports or fixes drift.
